## Summary
<h2>2026.9.1</h2>
<h3>Highlights</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Gateway restart recovery:</strong> preserve admitted turns across repeated Gateway restarts so restart-safe runs continue through each checkpoint and deliver their final response. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5262360470" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/130491" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130491/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130491">#130491</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/jalehman/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/jalehman">@jalehman</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Gateway config-write reliability:</strong> keep committed config writes pending through watcher handoff so same-write reloads settle against the observed generation instead of failing during source transfer. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5274647885" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/131515" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/131515/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/131515">#131515</a>)</li>
<li><strong>Codex managed runtime:</strong> update the bundled Codex runtime to 0.150.1 across Linux, macOS, and Windows while retaining platform-specific package resolution and current compaction behavior. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5263855040" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/130685" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130685/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130685">#130685</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/vincentkoc/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/vincentkoc">@vincentkoc</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Linux installation reliability:</strong> provision the stable Node 24 LTS stream and constrain RPM installs to the configured NodeSource repository so Linux setup cannot select an incompatible prerelease. <a href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130369" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130369/hovercard">PR 130369</a>. Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/RomneyDa/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/RomneyDa">@RomneyDa</a> and <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/vincentkoc/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/vincentkoc">@vincentkoc</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Worker recovery:</strong> re-arm admission-deadline launches, terminalize dead-worker turns, and defer debris cleanup so interrupted delegated work settles visibly. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5261833840" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/130446" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130446/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130446">#130446</a>)</li>
<li><strong>Control UI file safety:</strong> preserve confirmed agent-file saves during overlapping reads and refreshes while keeping newer drafts intact. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5262065868" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/130468" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130468/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130468">#130468</a>)</li>
<li><strong>Model browsing reliability:</strong> keep model discovery available after automatic plugin activation instead of losing the selected provider catalog. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5262200991" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/130481" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130481/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130481">#130481</a>)</li>
</ul>
<h3>Changes</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Audit decisions:</strong> record generic tool-action decisions at their authoritative execution boundary for clearer operator diagnostics. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5261046544" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/130358" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130358/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130358">#130358</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/joshavant/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/joshavant">@joshavant</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Codex app-server compatibility:</strong> carry Codex 0.150.1 collaboration, status, and activity protocol additions through the managed bridge without weakening exact platform package checks. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5263855040" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/130685" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130685/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130685">#130685</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/vincentkoc/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/vincentkoc">@vincentkoc</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Control UI appearance:</strong> save appearance preferences per user profile so shared browsers no longer collapse everyone onto one visual setup. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5260953058" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/130340" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130340/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130340">#130340</a>)</li>
<li><strong>Model selection scopes:</strong> add configurable model-selection scopes while preserving the canonical provider and runtime policy path. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5221114259" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/127813" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/127813/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/127813">#127813</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/Marvinthebored/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/Marvinthebored">@Marvinthebored</a> and <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/Peetiegonzalez/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/Peetiegonzalez">@Peetiegonzalez</a>.</li>
</ul>
<h3>Fixes</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Gateway restart recovery:</strong> retain admitted restart-safe runs across cleanup and repeated restarts, preserving checkpoints and final delivery. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5262360470" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/130491" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130491/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130491">#130491</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/jalehman/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/jalehman">@jalehman</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Gateway config watcher handoff:</strong> preserve pending config writes until the watcher observes the committed generation, preventing <code>config.patch</code> from failing during same-write source handoff. Fixes <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5273967715" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/131405" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/131405/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/131405">#131405</a>. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5274647885" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/131515" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/131515/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/131515">#131515</a>)</li>
<li><strong>Linux installer runtime selection:</strong> use stable Node 24 LTS provisioning and scope RPM installs to NodeSource so fresh Linux installs cannot select an incompatible prerelease. <a href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130369" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130369/hovercard">PR 130369</a>. Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/RomneyDa/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/RomneyDa">@RomneyDa</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Worker admission recovery:</strong> re-arm eligible launches after admission deadlines and terminalize dead-worker turns instead of leaving delegated work unresolved. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5261833840" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/130446" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130446/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130446">#130446</a>)</li>
<li><strong>Control UI agent files:</strong> keep confirmed saves and file metadata intact when older reads or list refreshes finish later. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5262065868" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/130468" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130468/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130468">#130468</a>)</li>
<li><strong>Model catalog activation:</strong> preserve model browsing after automatic provider-plugin activation. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5262200991" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/130481" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130481/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130481">#130481</a>)</li>
</ul>

## Why It Matters
Source signal: <h2>2026.9.1</h2>
<h3>Highlights</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Gateway restart recovery:</strong> preserve admitted turns across repeated Gateway restarts so restart-safe runs continue through each checkpoint and deliver their final response. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5262360470" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/130491" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130491/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130491">#130491</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/jalehman/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/jalehman">@jalehman</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Gateway config-write reliability:</strong> keep committed config writes pending through watcher handoff so same-write reloads settle against the observed generation instead of failing during source transfer. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5274647885" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/131515" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/131515/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/131515">#131515</a>)</li>
<li><strong>Codex managed runtime:</strong> update the bundled Codex runtime to 0.150.1 across Linux, macOS, and Windows while retaining platform-specific package resolution and current compaction behavior. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5263855040" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/130685" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130685/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130685">#130685</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/vincentkoc/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/vincentkoc">@vincentkoc</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Linux installation reliability:</strong> provision the stable Node 24 LTS stream and constrain RPM installs to the configured NodeSource repository so Linux setup cannot select an incompatible prerelease. <a href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130369" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130369/hovercard">PR 130369</a>. Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/RomneyDa/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/RomneyDa">@RomneyDa</a> and <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/vincentkoc/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/vincentkoc">@vincentkoc</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Worker recovery:</strong> re-arm admission-deadline launches, terminalize dead-worker turns, and defer debris cleanup so interrupted delegated work settles visibly. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5261833840" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/130446" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130446/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130446">#130446</a>)</li>
<li><strong>Control UI file safety:</strong> preserve confirmed agent-file saves during overlapping reads and refreshes while keeping newer drafts intact. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5262065868" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/130468" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130468/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130468">#130468</a>)</li>
<li><strong>Model browsing reliability:</strong> keep model discovery available after automatic plugin activation instead of losing the selected provider catalog. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5262200991" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/130481" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130481/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130481">#130481</a>)</li>
</ul>
<h3>Changes</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Audit decisions:</strong> record generic tool-action decisions at their authoritative execution boundary for clearer operator diagnostics. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5261046544" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/130358" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130358/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130358">#130358</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/joshavant/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/joshavant">@joshavant</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Codex app-server compatibility:</strong> carry Codex 0.150.1 collaboration, status, and activity protocol additions through the managed bridge without weakening exact platform package checks. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5263855040" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/130685" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130685/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130685">#130685</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/vincentkoc/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/vincentkoc">@vincentkoc</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Control UI appearance:</strong> save appearance preferences per user profile so shared browsers no longer collapse everyone onto one visual setup. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5260953058" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/130340" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130340/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130340">#130340</a>)</li>
<li><strong>Model selection scopes:</strong> add configurable model-selection scopes while preserving the canonical provider and runtime policy path. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5221114259" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/127813" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/127813/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/127813">#127813</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/Marvinthebored/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/Marvinthebored">@Marvinthebored</a> and <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/Peetiegonzalez/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/Peetiegonzalez">@Peetiegonzalez</a>.</li>
</ul>
<h3>Fixes</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Gateway restart recovery:</strong> retain admitted restart-safe runs across cleanup and repeated restarts, preserving checkpoints and final delivery. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5262360470" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/130491" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130491/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130491">#130491</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/jalehman/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/jalehman">@jalehman</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Gateway config watcher handoff:</strong> preserve pending config writes until the watcher observes the committed generation, preventing <code>config.patch</code> from failing during same-write source handoff. Fixes <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5273967715" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/131405" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/131405/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/131405">#131405</a>. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5274647885" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/131515" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/131515/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/131515">#131515</a>)</li>
<li><strong>Linux installer runtime selection:</strong> use stable Node 24 LTS provisioning and scope RPM installs to NodeSource so fresh Linux installs cannot select an incompatible prerelease. <a href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130369" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130369/hovercard">PR 130369</a>. Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/RomneyDa/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/RomneyDa">@RomneyDa</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Worker admission recovery:</strong> re-arm eligible launches after admission deadlines and terminalize dead-worker turns instead of leaving delegated work unresolved. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5261833840" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/130446" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130446/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130446">#130446</a>)</li>
<li><strong>Control UI agent files:</strong> keep confirmed saves and file metadata intact when older reads or list refreshes finish later. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5262065868" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/130468" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130468/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130468">#130468</a>)</li>
<li><strong>Model catalog activation:</strong> preserve model browsing after automatic provider-plugin activation. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5262200991" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/130481" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130481/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130481">#130481</a>)</li>
</ul>
